    @pytest.fixture
    def site() -> Iterator[Site]:
        """Serve a fresh fixture site for one test, and stop it afterwards."""
    
        served = Site()
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self) -> None:
                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))
                served.requests.append(request)
                gated = served.gate(request) if served.gate else None
                if gated is not None:
                    self.send_response(gated.status)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", gated.content_type)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(gated.body)))
                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10470edb0>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:

                    self.end_headers()

                self.send_response(response.status)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x1047afad0>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

    @pytest.fixture
    def site() -> Iterator[Site]:
        """Serve a fresh fixture site for one test, and stop it afterwards."""
    
        served = Site()
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self) -> None:
                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))
                served.requests.append(request)
                gated = served.gate(request) if served.gate else None
                if gated is not None:
                    self.send_response(gated.status)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", gated.content_type)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(gated.body)))
                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.

            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x1047be990>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return
    
>       server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """

        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return
    
>       server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))

                    self.end_headers()

                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return
    
>       server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

        served = Site()
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self) -> None:
                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))
                served.requests.append(request)
                gated = served.gate(request) if served.gate else None
                if gated is not None:
                    self.send_response(gated.status)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", gated.content_type)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(gated.body)))
                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)

                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x105b85760>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
>       self.socket.bind(self.server_address)
E       PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted

../../.local/share/uv/python/cpython-3.12.13-macos-aarch64-none/lib/python3.12/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
______ ERROR at setup of test_urls_differing_only_in_case_are_both_saved _______
[gw2] darwin -- Python 3.12.13 /Users/thomas/Projects/skills-388-391/.scratch-390/uv-cache/builds-v0/.tmpduVqix/bin/python

    @pytest.fixture
    def site() -> Iterator[Site]:
        """Serve a fresh fixture site for one test, and stop it afterwards."""
    
        served = Site()
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self) -> None:
                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))
                served.requests.append(request)
                gated = served.gate(request) if served.gate else None
                if gated is not None:
                    self.send_response(gated.status)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", gated.content_type)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(gated.body)))
                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return
    
>       server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

        served = Site()
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self) -> None:
                host = self.headers.get("Host", "")
                request = Request(host, self.path, dict(self.headers))
                served.requests.append(request)
                gated = served.gate(request) if served.gate else None
                if gated is not None:
                    self.send_response(gated.status)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", gated.content_type)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(gated.body)))
                    for name, value in gated.headers.items():
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(gated.body)
                    return
                responses = served.host_routes.get(
                    (host.rpartition(":")[0], self.path)
                ) or served.routes.get(self.path)
                if not responses:
                    self.send_response(404)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                response = responses.pop(0) if len(responses) > 1 else responses[0]
                if not_modified(response, dict(self.headers)):
                    self.send_response(304)
                    for name in VALIDATORS:
                        if name in response.headers:
                            self.send_header(name, response.headers[name])
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                self.send_response(response.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", response.content_type)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response.body)))
                for name, value in response.headers.items():
                    self.send_header(name, value)
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(response.body)
    
            def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:
                return

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
